Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the homotopy framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 421. The resulting operator achieves bound O(495/n^3).

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to homotopy theory, we decompose the problem into 421 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 495. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.
